Terra has 116,000 samples on 16 lines: 1.0 million coarse triangles of walls, about 23 MB on the GPU, and at exit 112 at 1500 the whole walls of 7 chunks, 0.44 million triangles and about 10 MB, where all of them whole were 5.5 million triangles and about 132 MB; `hex.PlanetMemory` logs both (at 26 bytes a triangle, with the fifth UV of the walls). The lines take about 34 ms to build and the coarse meshes 60 ms. `hex.Wireframe` draws their triangles as of no terrain (`hex.WireframeTarget` 0 or 2): a copy of every chunk while it draws every cell (`WallWireChunks`, `BuildWallWires`, built again with every change of the surface, the walls themselves hidden by `hex.Wireframe` 2, `bWallsHiddenByWire`), and around the cursor by `hex.WireframeScope` the walls along the cell under it and its neighbors in their colors (`HexPlanetWalls::BuildNear`, the samples listed for a cell within 1.1 of a cell radius of its middle, so a void cell shows the walls along its sides). They draw with `M_HexUndergroundWall` (`WALL_CODE` of `create_planet_assets.py`): the rock layer of the void (`RockLayer`) with its `LayerParams`, times `CaveWallColor` (about 2.5, as a steep wall draws the dark photo scan of the basalt alone), lighter and warmer on the stalactites by `CaveWallRibTint` (by how much of one stands out, V of the second UV), streaked down the faces by a noise of the direction from the center, which a vertical line of a wall keeps (`CaveWallStreaks`), in patches of tone (`CaveWallTone`) and layers (`CaveWallStrata`), split along its joints into slabs `CaveWallFacetCell` across and `CaveWallFacetStretch` times as tall (a cellular pattern whose third axis is the height above the floor), each leaning its normal its own way (`CaveWallFacetTilt`), with cracks between them in patches (`CaveWallCrack*`), fading out where a slab covers a few pixels (a pattern of round cells looked like masonry), all by its style: broken rock shows its joints and layers most and thin pale veins in patches (`CaveWallVein*`; level lines of a noise everywhere drew white scribbles), flowstone is lighter, warmer and smoother (`CaveWallFlowTint`, `CaveWallFlowRoughness`); in regions (`CaveWallAltShare`, `CaveWallAltScale`) it is another rock, `CaveWallAltLayerName` (OchreRock) tinted by `CaveWallAltColor` (`CaveWallAltLayer`, -1 without it), and rusty stains run down it (`CaveWallStain*`), darker in its hollows (`CaveWallCavityShade`, U of the first UV) and towards its crest (`CaveWallTopShade` from `CaveWallTopStart`, as the light of a cave comes from its floor) and down the outer face below the floor by the curve of the cliffs below it (`FloorRadius`, `AbyssFadeStart`, `AbyssFadeEnd`, `AbyssDarkness`), so its lower edge, which a lake or lava lets one see, meets them without a seam (a darkening of its own, by `AbyssDepthHalf`, left it lit there over a black cliff); its foot and the floor before it (U of the second UV) are the floor of the passages, the terrain layer of their biome (`FloorLayer`) with `CaveFloorTint`, `CaveFloorBrightness` and its pools, the edge moved by `CaveWallFloorNoise`, so the wall rises out of the floor, and it takes the pools of light of the floor (`CaveWallGlowLight` up `CaveWallGlowRise` of the inner face, the foot as the floor beside it: `AHexPlanet::UpdateWallGlow` writes for each sample of the walls the pool of light of the floor of its passage where it stands, as the floor draws it, into `WallGlowTexture`, a pixel per sample, line after line, each line from the start of a row of 2048 so an edit of one line moves the pixels of the others only when it takes a row more or less, and the fifth UV of a vertex, which `UHexPlanetMeshComponent` gives only the walls, 40 bytes a vertex, holds the pixel of its sample, which the vertex shader reads, so the pixels between two samples blend their pools; `HashChunk` takes the pixels too; until 2026-11-10 the foot of a wall in a crystal cavern was a tan strip), and U of the second UV below 0 is how much a point is the outer face (`FLocal::Smooth`, as no row is both). The glowing moss of the rims grows on the foot and up `CaveWallMossTop` of the inner face (`CaveWallMoss`, in the patches and speckles of `CaveMoss*`), never on the outer face, and on a layer with walls the moss of the floor along the rims is off (`WallsShown` of `M_HexUnderground`, from `SetBiomeLayers`), as it ran along the sides of the hexes and poked out from under a wall where its line cut a corner. Each void gives the walls along it a height, a factor on `WallHeight` (`FSample::Height`, from `VoidHeights` of `HexPlanetWalls::Build`): set by hand (`AHexPlanet::SetWallHeights`, `WallHeightOverrides`, in `underground/walls.json`, a line per void, `HexPlanetFile::SaveWalls`, written with the levels, deleted when empty, a void that became a passage left out), else by its style (`GetStyleWallHeight`), `WallPoolHeight` along a void of lava or a lake (`EHexVoidStyle`) and 1 along the mist, always within `WallHeightMin` (0.2, a low curb, so no passage opens onto the abyss, as the developer asked on 2026-11-10) and `WallHeightMax` (2) (`UHexPlanetConfig::ClampWallHeight`). The heights, and how much of the void is lava or a lake (`FSample::Lava` and `Lake`), ease from one void to the next over `WallHeightBlend` each way along the line (once `WallPoolBlend`, which a `CoreRedirects` line of `DefaultEngine.ini` carries over), as an S between the lowest and highest around; each sample takes the passage and void of the stretch of the line it lies on (the nearer point of the line gave the second half of each side the next void, which moved the parapets half a side on until 2026-11-10). On top of that the walls along `WallHeightJitterShare` of the voids stand higher or lower by up to `WallHeightJitter` of their height and the rest as they are, by a hash of the void (`FSample::Jitter`, eased along the line as the heights are and never making a wall a parapet), so neighboring walls differ a little, as the developer asked on 2026-11-10. A lower wall is thinner, with less overhang and smaller stalactites (`GetParapet`), a parapet of `WallPoolThickness` of its thickness at `WallPoolHeight`, so the pool shows from the passage, and below that it narrows towards its line, its foot too (`FWallFrame::Presence`, `FLocal::FromSmooth`), which the walls carry down to nothing at 0 (the `Underground.Walls` test), though the planet never gives them less than `WallHeightMin`; a wall lower than a parapet keeps no rims on its face, and one nearly gone none at its foot (`PlaceOnWalls`, `HexPlanetWalls::GetPresence`). The fourth UV carries the lava and the lake to the material, where lava lights the rock: the parapet by `CaveWallLavaGlow`, fading up it and less on its inner face, the outer face as the cliffs of its void (`LavaCliffLight`, `LavaCliffGlow`, `LavaCliffTint`, up to `LavaCliffReach` above the floor of the void, `LevelHeight` + `DeepDepth` below the passages), and the floor at its foot as the passage there (`GlowPoolEdge` of `LavaPool` in the color of `LavaLightColor`, by `GlowPoolStrength` and `GlowPoolLight`); a glow added whatever the rock drew it as a plate of light. A change of biomes that changes the style of a void, or of the heights, builds the walls again at once (`AHexPlanet::SetCells`, `bVoidStyleChanged`, `bWallHeightsChanged`), or leaves that to a rebuild of the levels that waits, which builds them, and the tooltips of the cave biomes of the palette say that those of voids take only voids (`UHexPlanetPainter::GetBiomeTip`); underground `hex.PlanetPaint biome` 2 is the lake and 3 the lava. Masked: around the line from the camera to the point under the cursor (`WallFadeCenter`, `AHexPlanet::SetFadeFocus` from `AHexPlanetPlayerController`) a wall dissolves where it stands in front of that point (`WALL_FADE_CODE`, `CaveWallFade*`), keeping `CaveWallFadeKeep` of its pixels by a noise that changes every frame, while the shadow pass and ray tracing keep it whole (`ShadowReplace`, `RayTracingQualitySwitch`); `hex.PlanetWallFade` 0 turns it off and 2 takes the point of the screen at `hex.PlanetWallFadeScreenY` of its height instead of the cursor, for screenshots. On a layer with walls a rim of `RimSeat` WallFoot or WallFace (`EHexRimSeat`, `UHexPlanetObjects::PlaceOnWalls`) stands at `RimSideShare` of the samples, each by a hash of where it lies and the Id: small spires (`CaveRimSpires`) and fallen stones and pebbles (`CaveWallRubble`) on the rounded foot and the floor before it, `RimCornerBoost` times more in the inside corners of the walls, where their line turns onto the passage (`FSample::InnerReach`), and rocks, stalactites (hanging, `bRimHang`), crystals (`DA_HexObject_CaveWallCrystals`) and carved blocks out of the inner face at `RimWallHeights` of the wall (`HexPlanetWalls::GetInnerFace`); the covers and decor keep `GetFootReach` off the line (`GetDistanceToLine`, 0 beyond it). `hex.PlanetWalls 0` hides them; at 1280x720 on the machine they were made on they cost about 0.08 ms of GPU at Terra exit 112 at 1500 with their LODs (3.26 / 3.18 ms with `hex.PlanetWalls` 1 and 0, measured in one session; 0.11 ms when all were whole, 0.05 ms before the styles) and 0.06 ms of the render thread, nothing measurable at 4000 (3.38 / 3.38) and about 0.07 ms at 12000 (2.81 / 2.74), where they were not drawn before. What stands on a wall (the rims of `RimSeat` WallFoot and WallFace) draws with `M_HexPlanetCaveWall` (`AHexPlanet::WallObjectMaterialAsset`) in place of the material of its set, the cave objects' material, masked with `WALL_FADE_CODE`, so it dissolves with the walls (`UHexPlanetObjects::SetFadeFocus`, from `SetFadeFocus`; whole in the shadow pass and ray tracing).
- The air of the underground (`bMotes` of `UHexPlanetConfig`, on in `DA_HexUnderground` by a migration of `create_planet_assets.py`): `UHexPlanetMotes`, made when the layer is built, holds `MoteCount` quads of `MoteMesh` with `M_HexPlanetMote` (additive, unlit, without shadows, ray tracing or distance fields), each standing once at a seeded spot of a box `MoteBox` wide around the component, which follows the point the view through the middle of the screen meets the tops of the passages in steps of an eighth of the box, so its bounds cover it. `MOTE_OFFSET_CODE` wraps each mote in a box of the world around `MoteOrigin` (with `MoteDrift` and a wobble), so it stays put in the world as the box follows the camera, and lays it along its direction from the center from `MoteHeightBelow` below the tops of the passages to `MoteHeightAbove` above them, so every mote lies over the passages on any part of the globe, in a quad facing the camera never under `MoteMinPixels` (`MotePixelScale` from the field of view and the viewport); it fades at the faces of the box, deeper in a chasm by `AbyssDepthHalf`, next to the camera and as the camera is `MoteFadeStart` to `MoteFadeEnd` from that point, past which both components are hidden. Spores (`MoteAnchored` 1) rise `SporeRise` over `SporeLife`, swirling `SporeSwirl` around the up of their cell, from hashed spots within `SporeSpread` of a cell radius of the middles of the cells within `SporeRadius` whose kinds have `Spores` (`UHexPlanetObjects::GetCellSpores`, nearest first, at most `SporeMax`), in the glow color of their kind times `SporeBrightness`, gathered again every half second and placed only when those cells changed. A spore stands at its instance, whose position only the vertex shader has: in a pixel shader Object Position is that of the component, the root of the planet at the origin, which gave no light at all, so the emissive takes it from the vertices through a `VertexInterpolator`; at `SporeSize` 4 and `SporeMinPixels` 3, as at 2 and 1.5 their soft disc lit under a tenth of a pixel. The material pads the bounds of its instances by `max_world_position_offset_displacement`, which the script sets from `DA_HexUnderground` (`UHexPlanetConfig::GetMoteWpoBound`, also its scalar `MoteWpoBound`, by which the offset is clamped), and the component warns when the asset needs more. `hex.PlanetMotes 0` hides them; they cost nothing measurable (3.08 / 3.08 ms at Terra 1500, 2.68 / 2.68 at Islet 1400).
- Birds and bats fly as decor (`bFlocks` of `UHexPlanetConfig`, `UHexPlanetFlocks`, a component of `AHexPlanet` made with its layer), as the developer asked on 2026-11-10 for flying birds and gulls and bats underground, by particles or another way: meshes moved by code were chosen over Niagara, as they cost next to nothing, stay at their places in the world and have every value in the data asset. The kinds are `Flocks` of the config (`FHexFlockKind`, the defaults from `MakeDefaultFlockKinds`), each a mesh, a habitat (`EHexFlockHabitat`) and how it flies: Gulls circle over the sea beside land, each on a loop of its own, Birds keep together in flocks over forests (a cover whose `FarCover` is a half or more), Raptors soar over the mountains, and Bats flutter through the halls of the underground, the passages whose neighbors are all passages; a layer flies the kinds whose habitat it has. `CellShare` of the cells of a habitat are the home of a flock, by a hash of the cell (`HashUnit`, mixed by `MurmurFinalize32`, as the plain hashes of a run of cells fall in one narrow band and all pass a share or all fail it), so a flock is at the same place in every run, those of the land and the coast two cells away from any mountain (`IsHome`); every half second, or at once when the focus of the camera moved two cells, the homes within `FlockRadius` of it are gathered, nearest first, up to `FlockMax` animals of a kind (`Gather`, which logs the homes as Verbose). An animal flies a loop around its home that is a function of the time alone (`GetPosition`: `PathRadius`, `PathStretch`, `PathWander`, `Spread`, `HeightMin` to `HeightMax` above the highest top of the home and the cells around it, `Speed`, on one loop together with `bTogether` or each on its own), and `Update` sets the transforms of the instances every frame (`GetTransform`, then `BatchUpdateInstancesTransforms` and `MarkRenderInstancesDirty`, as marking the whole render state dirty would make the proxy anew every frame), each heading where it goes and leaning into its turn by `Bank`, so their bounds, shadows and motion vectors are right; a component of instances per kind, not in ray tracing. An instance is added where its animal flies and never scaled to nothing: one added at a scale near 0 was never drawn again, however it was moved afterwards, which cost a long search. `M_HexPlanetFlock` (`FLOCK_OFFSET_CODE` of `create_planet_assets.py`, whose argument `flocks` builds it alone) beats the wings: the first UV of a vertex is how far out on a wing it lies, and it swings about the line of the body `FlapRate` times a second by `FlapAngle`, each animal at its own pace by the custom data of its instance, gliding `GlideShare` of the time with its wings held `GlideAngle` above level. Past `FlockFadeStart` from the camera an animal shrinks, to nothing at `FlockFadeEnd`, and a camera further than that from its focus hides them all. The meshes, a few triangles each, flat shapes seen from above, come from `Tools/HexPlanetObjects/generate_planet_flocks.py` (`Saved/HexPlanetFlocks`), and `import_planet_flocks.py`, run with the commandlet, imports them to `/Game/HexPlanet/Flocks`. The animals are larger than life (`Size` of a kind, 1.6 to 2.2), as a gull of its true size is a dozen pixels from the closest camera, and `hex.PlanetFlockSize` tries sizes while the game runs; the meshes have one LOD with its screen size set by hand (`SetMeshLods`). `hex.PlanetFlocks 0` hides them; with 35 gulls in view and their shadows they cost nothing that could be measured on the machine they were made on (3.71 / 3.70 ms of GPU with `hex.PlanetFlocks` 1 and 0, the render thread within its noise).
- Heroes walk the planet (`UHexHeroes` in `Source/HexHero/Hero`, an object of the surface `AHexPlanet` that serves both layers, `AHexPlanet::GetHeroes`, updated from its `Tick` whichever layer is shown), as the developer agreed on 2026-11-10 and changed on 2026-11-11: several heroes, each of which lights the map around itself, moved by clicks of the left button while the game is played (see the game below). Every value is in `DA_HexHeroes` (`UHexHeroConfig`, `Content/HexPlanet/Heroes`, which `import_planet_heroes.py` creates only when missing, so tune it by hand; a missing one falls back to the defaults of the class with a warning): the rules, the classes (`FHexHeroClass`: the attributes a hero starts with, the chances of each to rise with a level, names for new heroes and an optional mesh; Knight and Wizard from `MakeDefaultHeroClasses`) and the players (`FHexHeroPlayer`, a name and a color, four by default). A hero (`FHexHero`) stands on a cell of a layer, one hero to a cell, with the points of movement it has left today and its attributes (Attack, Defense, Spell Power, Knowledge, mana, level, experience); its Attack, Defense, Spell Power and mana count in battles since 2026-11-12 (see the battles), and nothing levels up yet. The rules of movement: `MovePoints` (10) every day; a step onto a cell costs the factor of its level (1 on plains and on the passages of the underground, `HillCost` 1.5 on hills) times `MoveCostMultiplier` of its biome (`UHexBiomeConfig`, from `create_biome_assets.py`: grass and steppe 1, rock 1.25, sand and tundra 1.5, snow 2, swamp 2.5), times `RiverCrossCost` of `DA_HexPlanet` across a river without a bridge (`UHexPlanetRivers::GetCrossingFactor`); the sea, which waits for ships, mountains, the voids, a biome with `bBlocksHeroes`, a kind of object that blocks (`UHexPlanetObjects::BlocksMovement`: forests and the masses of the underground; the cover Rocks and the single Boulder of the decor blocked until 2026-11-11, when the developer said that a few small rocks on open ground, a lone stone, bones and other small things lying about must not ("таке каміння не блокує мувмент, скелети ... і інше дрібне сміття"), so a kind blocks only when it fills its cell; `MIGRATIONS` of `import_planet_objects.py` holds both) and a cell another hero stands on cannot be entered (`GetCellCost`, `GetStepCost`, `CanStand`, which says why). A step is taken only while the points left pay for it, so what is left over is lost with the day, and a step dearer than a whole day takes a day (`HexHeroPath::CanStep`, `Spend`). `HexHeroPath` is pure (the `HexHero.Hero.Path` and `HexHero.Hero.Spend` tests): `FindField` finds the cheapest ways from a cell by Dijkstra over the neighbors, within `PathMaxDays` of movement, keeping the cell found first among ways of one cost, so every run gives the same way, and `FindLayerField` does the same over both layers, which share a grid: a node is a cell of a layer (the layer times the number of cells plus the cell, `UHexHeroes::GetNode`), and a way may go from a cell to the same cell of the other layer where a link is given. From 2026-11-11 to 2026-11-12 no way ran over an entrance or exit it did not use ("шлях не обходить вхід"), while a hero stood on the entrance itself; since the entrances are built into mountains and walls the cell before one is ground as any other and ways run over it (the `HexHero.Hero.Path` test). A link costs what it is said to, nothing too: until 2026-11-12 the search took the least cost of a step for it, so what a hero reached with its last point on the other layer was left out of what it reaches today. So the way of a hero a person moves goes through the underground, as the developer asked on 2026-11-11 ("шлях через підземелля не будується, прохід підземелля коштує 1"): down an entrance, along the passages and up an exit, each for `EntranceCost`, nothing since 2026-11-12 (`FindFieldFor`, whose link is an entrance or exit that can be used, which its player has seen and on whose other side no hero it knows of stands; a hero the game moves goes through only where its player has seen both sides, as it keeps to what it explored). A cell of either layer may be clicked for a hero on the other (`FHexHero::TargetLayer`, `Send`, `Click` and `SetTarget` with a layer; the camera goes to a hero sent on the layer not shown, `OnHeroSent`, `AHexPlanetPlayerController::OnHeroSent`). The hero a person sent waits while the camera is on a trip between the layers (`UHexHeroes::SetViewTravelling`, which the camera pawn sets), and `AHexPlanetCameraPawn::ShowCell` asked for during a trip is done once it ends (`PendingShowLayer`), as the developer found on 2026-11-11 that the camera, still on its way to the layer of the hero, did not go down after it. The way of a hero, `Route`, stays the cells it enters in order, and a cell that comes again in it is the step through the entrance or exit there (`WalkWay`, `GetWayStepCost`, `StartNextStep`), after which a hero a person plays waits `EntrancePause` on the other side while the camera comes after it (`FHexHero::WalkWait`). The way shows on each layer that is built, to the pit, on from the stairs below and so on, its numbers on the layer shown (`UpdatePath`), and the panel says through how many entrances it goes. Where it goes through one it shows it, as the developer asked on 2026-11-12 ("візуалізірувати додатковою точкою та відрізком до прохода ... щось інше що буде символізувати прохід через гору - телепорт"): on the layer it leaves a line from the middle of the cell to the mouth (`GetEntranceMouth`) and there a hoop with a swirl of three arms in it (`PathPortalMesh`, `SM_PlanetHeroPortal`, `portal` of `generate_planet_heroes.py`), which stands upright in the mouth facing out of it, `PathPortalRadius` large with its middle `PathPortalLift` of that above the ground, and on the layer it comes to the same hoop in the mouth there and a line from it to the middle of the cell (`FLayerMarks::Portals`, `AddPortal` in `UpdatePath`), and the hoop shows which way the way goes through, as the developer asked the same day: `PathPortalLayers` (4) plain hoops without the swirl (`PathPortalRingMesh`, `SM_PlanetHeroPortalRing`, `FLayerMarks::Rings`) run from it along the way, one after the other, each over `PathPortalTravel` in `PathPortalPulseTime` seconds, growing smaller down to `PathPortalLayerScale` of the hoop and fading in and out on its way, out of the mouth where the way comes out and, mirrored, from the hoop on into the passage where the way goes in, drawn over the rock they run into (from before the mouth into it at first, which the developer called wrong; and 4 hoops in 2.8 s since they asked for slower and closer together, 2 in 1.4 s before). The material moves them: the fourth to sixth custom values of an instance of a mark are how far it runs along its own Z, from which share of the cycle and how much smaller it grows (`Travel`, `Phase`, `Shrink` of `HERO_MARK_OFFSET_CODE`, which draws one that runs against its Z nearer the camera by twice as far as it has run, and `HERO_MARK_OPACITY_CODE`, `MarkTravelSpeed`), 0 for a mark that stays. Tried before that and asked away by the developer: an arrow on the ground before the hoop, three hoops of three sizes that stood still, which read as a heap ("потрібно анімувати з рухом та прозорістю"), and triangles in place of the dots of the steps, which at the turns of a way did not tell where it went, so the steps are dots ("прибираєм трикутник лишаєм точки"). The hoop and the line of the mouth a hero came out of stay on its way until it stops (`FHexHero::CameOutCell`, as the cells it left do), as the developer found on 2026-11-12 that they went as soon as it had come through. Words after the file name of `import_planet_heroes.py` import only the meshes whose names hold one, and no rider, so the others stay as they are. An underground that was never shown has its levels but not its objects, so what blocks there is read from its `objects.json` meanwhile (`UHexPlanetObjects::FindSavedBlockers`, `IsBlockedUnbuilt`). The ways of the hero chosen are found again only when it moved or the map changed (`UHexHeroes::NoteMapChanged`, which `AHexPlanet::SetCells`, `OnRiversChanged` and `UHexPlanetObjects::SetCells` call, the cost of each cell found once a search; about 0.3 to 0.5 ms on Terra, logged as Verbose). While the game is played the way of the hero chosen to the cell it is pointed to (`FHexHero::TargetCell`, `SetTarget`) shows on the ground (`UpdatePath`): a line (`SM_PlanetHeroLine`, `PathLineWidth`) through a dot on each cell to a target on the last, yellow orange (`PathColor`) as far as it gets today and a darker orange (`PathFarColor`) beyond, see-through (`PathLineOpacity`, `PathMarkOpacity`, `PathBrightness`), and a red target (`PathBlockedColor`) on a cell it cannot reach, with why written over it (`FPathLabel::Text`: "Forest in the way", the kind by `UHexPlanetObjects::GetBlockerName`, as on 2026-11-11 the developer found no way shown to cells of the cover Rocks, which blocked then). The way lies on the ground under the hero and whatever stands on it, as the developer asked on 2026-11-11 (until then it was drawn over everything): the line is laid in pieces at most `PathLineStep` long, each between two points of the ground (`FindGroundPoint`, from the triangles of the two cells, kept in `GroundTriangles`, at most 256 cells), across the whole line so the pieces meet edge to edge, and a dot or target leans as the ground around the middle of its cell (`FindGroundUp`). The way starts at a dot of its own on the cell of the hero, as the developer asked on 2026-11-11 (the line began at the hero before), and that dot stays where the hero set out until it stops: the cells it left since (`FHexHero::Trail`, filled by `StartNextStep`, emptied when it stops or changes layer) stay on the way with the step it takes and the rest of it, so a way of one step, as a click of the right button gives, shows as well, and the start is not a new cell with every step, which the developer found it was the same day. Where a day ends on the way its number is drawn, and at the last cell the days the whole way takes when it is not walked today (`HexHeroPath::FindStepDays`, `GetPathLabels`, drawn on the screen by `SHexHeroPanel::OnPaint` over the projected marks, `PathLabel*`), and the panel of the heroes says what the way costs (`GetHoverText`). Where the hero chosen gets today shows on the ground, as the developer asked on 2026-11-12 ("малювати обводку по гексам куди може дійти гравець за даний день", with the ground outside a little darker, "як накласти прозоре чорне"): while the game is played, it is the turn of the person and the hero can take a step, `UHexHeroes::UpdateReach` finds the cells its points pay for (`FindFieldFor` with them as the most a way may cost, on both layers, through the entrances), adds the mines, mills and castles it can visit from one of them, a castle with its three cells, the castle it guards, and the cell of another hero beside one of them, so that cuts no hole, and gives each layer its cells (`AHexPlanet::SetReach`); a hero that can take no step shows none (`bShowReach` of `DA_HexHeroes` turns it off). As they asked, it is found when a hero is chosen and when it stops, so while it walks what was shown stays, and also when its points or the map change while it stands. `CellReach` of a planet is a texture as `CellMarks`, a pixel per cell: red the bits of the sides of the edge of those cells, in both cells of a side, and green 255 in them, which `MARK_CODE` draws in the surfaces of both layers and the grid of the sea (the argument `marks` of `create_planet_assets.py`): a line along the edge as an outline is (`ReachLineColor`, `ReachLineWidth`, `ReachLineMinPixels`, `ReachLineOpacity`, `ReachLineGlow`), a soft light of its color inside it that fades inwards (`ReachGlowWidth`, `ReachGlowOpacity`), the ground outside `ReachOutsideShade` (0.3) of the way to `ReachOutsideColor`, black, and the ground inside `ReachInsideTint` (0) to `ReachInsideColor`, all `Highlight|Reach` of the config of the layer, coming and going over `ReachFadeTime` (`ReachShown`, `AHexPlanet::UpdateReach`). Only the ground takes the tone: not the sea, nor what stands on a cell, and underground the foot of a wall covers the line along the edge of a passage. Its GPU cost, a texel more a pixel of the ground, is not measured. A hero keeps the cell it is pointed to until it is there, and the way to it is found again whenever the map or the heroes changed, so a way left unwalked while another hero was moved is the best one when the player comes back to it. `Send` sets it walking, cell by cell at `WalkSpeed` cells a second, setting out and stopping smoothly, as the developer asked on 2026-11-11 (`FHexHero::Speed`: it reaches that speed over `WalkStartTime` and brakes over `WalkStopTime` to a stop where its way or its points end, `CountStepsAhead`, never slower than `WalkMinSpeedShare` of it), paying for each step as it starts it (`StartNextStep`, which asks the cost again, so a way blocked meanwhile stops it), turning where it walks (`TurnSpeed`) and bobbing (`WalkBob`) on the ground under it (`AHexPlanet::FindGroundRadius` of the cell there), until the way or its points end; an order to a hero that walks takes over after the step it is on. Until 2026-11-12 a way that ended with a step onto an entrance or exit that can be used took the hero through to the same cell of the other layer (since then it goes through only when sent to the mouth, see the entrances) (`Travel`, `PassEntrance`, for `EntranceCost`: nothing, as the developer decided on 2026-11-12, the step onto the cell costing what any step does ("перехід на гекс з проходом коштує 1 але сам трансфер між поверхнями безкоштовний"), after a point of movement from 2026-11-11, with which a hero that spent its last point on that step stopped on the cell and did not go through; refused while a hero stands on the other side; a hero pointed nowhere that stands on such a cell goes through when it is sent, `UHexHeroes::Go`, so the key that sends it takes it to and fro between the layers for no points, as they asked the same day; pointing a hero to its own cell and sending it does the same), where it stands before the mouth of the rock there (`GetStandDirection`, `AHexPlanet::GetEntrancePlace`), and the camera goes with the hero chosen (`OnHeroTravelled`, `AHexPlanetCameraPawn::ShowCell`); the camera also glides after it while it walks (`bCameraFollows`, `AHexPlanetCameraPawn::Follow`), until the person moves it with the keys, a drag, the middle or the right button, which takes the camera over until the hero stops, as the developer asked on 2026-11-12 (`TakeFollow`, `bFollowTaken`; a click on a mark at the edge of the screen does not), while the edge of the screen never takes it over and scrolls only once the person has. The players move in turns (see below). The game is played or not (`UHexHeroes::IsPlaying`, `StartPlay`, `StopPlay`; `AHexPlanetPlayerController::SetPlayMode`), as the developer asked on 2026-11-11: P (`IA_HexPlayMode`) starts and stops it as B does the editing, the two never run together, and both are buttons at the top left (`SHexModeBar`, the palette below it, `GetPaletteTop`), with a third beside them since 2026-11-12, when the developer asked for a button for U: it takes the camera to the other layer at the same place (`TravelToLayer`) and names the layer it goes to. It starts only with a hero on the planet, where the heroes stand, not from day 1 (New game of the panel does that, `Restart`), and a hero is always chosen while it is played: when the one chosen leaves the planet another is (`KeepChosen`), and without any the game is lost and stops. `IA_HexHeroSelect` (the left button) is a click, a press and a release within `ClickTolerance`, as held and moved the button drags the planet (`AHexPlanetCameraPawn::OnHeroSelectCompleted`; several actions of one mapping context may share a key), and `AHexPlanetPlayerController::ClickGame` gives it to `UHexHeroes::Click` with the hero under the cursor, by its body (`FindHeroAlongRay`, `PickRadius`, `PickHeight`) else by its cell: on another hero it chooses it, on a cell it points the hero chosen there, so its way shows, a second click on that cell sends it (`Go`), and a click, the key that sends it or the right button stops a hero that walks, at the end of its step (`UHexHeroes::Stop`). A click of the right button, which held and moved turns the camera, sends the hero chosen to the cell under the cursor at once (`AHexPlanetCameraPawn::OnTurnCompleted`, `AHexPlanetPlayerController::OrderGame`), as the developer asked on 2026-11-11 while still thinking over the controls. Two clicks at one place within `DoubleClickTime` on a hero choose it and bring the camera to it (`ShowHero`); while the game is played the double click on an entrance no longer takes the camera through it, as two clicks there send a hero. Space (`IA_HexHeroSend`) walks the way shown, and with none shown and no entrance under the hero brings the camera to it, as the developer asked on 2026-11-12, Tab (`IA_HexNextHero`) chooses the next hero a person moves and shows it, Enter (`IA_HexEndTurn`) ends the turn, and Z (`IA_HexSkipHero`, also Skip of the panel and `hex.Hero skip [Hero]`) leaves the hero chosen for today, as the developer asked on 2026-11-11 (`UHexHeroes::Skip`, `FHexHero::bSkipped`): it stops, the next hero that can move is chosen, and Tab and that choice pass over it until the next day or until the person sends it again; the panel marks it skipped. When the hero chosen stops with no points for another step, or its way ends for want of them, the next hero that can still move is chosen and shown (`Arrive`, `CanMove`, `FindNextAble`, `OnHeroChosen`), after `NextHeroDelay` seconds (1.2, `UpdatePendingNext`), so the player sees that it used its movement and where it stopped, as the developer asked on 2026-11-11; a click, another hero chosen or the end of the day in that pause keeps the choice. The developer tried the clicks and Tab of 2026-11-10 with the mouse; the rules of 2026-11-11 Claude tried through `hex.Hero` alone. `SHexHeroPanel`, shown while the game is played, lists the heroes at the bottom right with the color of their player and the movement they have left, (and `SHexEdgeMarkers`, which the developer asked for on 2026-11-12, marks at the edges of the screen the heroes and castles of the person that are out of view: one on the edge in the direction of each on the layer shown (those of the other layer have none since 2026-11-12, when the developer found their marks pointed the wrong way and asked them away), `AHexPlanetPlayerController::LocateForMarker`, towards where it would show or, for one behind the planet, the way over the ground from what the camera looks at; a disc with a banner for a hero and a square with battlements for a castle, in the color of the player, larger and ringed for the one chosen. The mark under the cursor shows its name; marks in a heap move apart along the edge while the cursor is on them, each taking the room its name needs, around the one the cursor came to first, which stays where it was drawn then (`AnchorOffset`: taken to where it points, a mark the one chosen had moved off itself went from under the cursor, the heap closed and it came back, so it jumped up and down, as the developer found on 2026-11-12), and the mark of the one chosen always keeps the others off itself, in a heap moved apart too. The widget handles no input: `ClickGame` asks it what lies under the cursor, `GetHovered`, and a click there chooses and shows that hero or castle; it stands over every other widget of the game (ZOrder 100, below the black of a trip, and gone while the menu or a dialog is open, `IsBlocked`) and is hit while the cursor is on its marks (`GetMarksVisibility`), so a click on a mark that lies over Edit or Play is the mark's and not the button's, as the developer asked on 2026-11-12 ("іконки напрямків - найвищий рівень по вводу"), Slate passing the click it leaves unhandled on up to the viewport and the game; while the cursor is on a mark or on a heap that moved apart the camera does not scroll at the edge of the screen (`IsCursorOnMarks`, `AHexPlanetPlayerController::IsCursorOnUi`, which the panel of a castle under the cursor makes true too, in `AHexPlanetCameraPawn`), as the developer asked on 2026-11-12, and then for everything that is clicked ("любе що клікається - вище по інпути, якщо нічого немає - можем скролитись"): `AHexCameraPawn::GetEdgeScrollInput`, which both maps use, gives nothing while the cursor is on a button, a check box, a spin box, a slider, a text box or a scroll bar of any panel (`IsCursorOnClickable`, by the types of the widgets Slate finds under the cursor inside the viewport of the game, looked up only in the zone of the edge), while labels and the backgrounds of panels let it scroll. A glide of the planet camera that was asked for (`ShowCell`, `FocusOn`, `FocusCell`, the middle button) is not stopped by the cursor it left at the edge: the edge scrolls again only once the mouse has moved `EdgeScrollHoldDistance` (12 pixels) of `DA_HexPlanetCamera` from where it was then (`HoldEdgeScroll`, `bEdgeScrollHeld`), as the developer found that a click on a mark sent the camera off, the mark left the cursor, and the scroll of the edge stopped the camera before it was there ("іконка тікає, а мішка на скролді - це зупиняє камеру і вона не підлітає"). `hex.Mouse <X> <Y>` puts the cursor at a point of the viewport and `hex.Mouse` logs what lies under it and whether the edge scrolls there, as test runs have no mouse. Its sizes are constants of `SHexEdgeMarkers.cpp`, not yet in a data asset) a click choosing and showing one, the attributes of the one chosen, what the way shown costs, and the buttons that end the turn and start a new game, and at the top what the person who plays (`GetShroudPlayer`) has of each resource and gets a day, whatever is chosen (it took the player of the hero chosen until 2026-11-11, so it was empty with a castle chosen). `heroes.json` of a planet (`FHexHeroLine`: id, name, class, player, layer, cell, and `army`, the stacks it starts with, `FHexStackLine`: faction, creature by Id, upgraded, count, there only when it was set, `hex.Hero startarmy [Hero|all]`, `UHexHeroes::KeepArmyAsStart`, which makes the army a hero has now, of the one chosen without a name, the one it starts with; `HexPlanetFile::SaveHeroes`, a line per hero sorted by id, deleted when there are none, and a line `"ai": [1]` of the players the game moves, there only when there are any; the `HexHero.Hero.File` test) holds where the heroes stand when a game starts: the save of the planet writes it, and placing or removing a hero leaves the planet unsaved ("Heroes and mines not saved"), until Undo is back at what the file holds (`SavedLines`). Where they walked to and the day are not saved yet, so each run starts there, and `hex.Hero restart` goes back to it. The Heroes tab of the palette on either layer (`EHexPaintTab::Heroes`, `CaveHeroes`, `EHexPaintTool::Hero`, `UHexPlanetPainter::PaintHero`) places a hero of the player and class chosen with a click and takes one away with Ctrl+click, each a step of Undo (`FHexPaintStep::OldHeroes`, `NewHeroes`, the lines of all heroes, which `UHexHeroes::SetLines` puts back; a hero whose line is the same stays where it walked to). `hex.Hero <list|play|stop|add <Cell> [Class] [Player]|remove <Hero>|select <Hero>|next|click <Cell>|target <Cell|off>|go|send <Cell> [Hero]|ways <Cell> [Rings]|ai <Player> [on|off]|points <Amount> [Hero]|skip [Hero]|endturn|restart>` drives them from the console and from `-ExecCmds`, a hero by its Id or name and a cell by its number, `focus` for the one the camera looks at, or `focus:<Steps>[:<Side>]` for one that many steps from it; `click` does what the left button does on a cell, `target` and `go` what a click and Space do, `ways` logs for each cell up to `Rings` around one what the way of the hero chosen there costs or why it has none (`LogWays`), for finding out why a click shows no way, and `points` gives the hero chosen, or the one named, more points of movement, or fewer with a negative amount (`AddMovePoints`), for trying the game. A hero is a mesh component of the planet of its layer, so it hides with it (`UpdateBody`). Since 2026-11-11, when the developer asked to find meshes of a horse and a man, simplify them and animate them ("мув та стоп, початок мува, реакція на вибір"), it is an animated rider: `SK_PlanetRider` (`RiderMesh` of `DA_HexHeroes`, a skeletal mesh, `RiderScale` times its size before `HeroScale` and turned by `RiderYaw`, -90, as the mesh comes in facing -Y; at 90 the horse ran backwards), built by `Tools/HexPlanetHeroes/blender_build_rider.py` (run with Blender; the argument `preview` renders frames of its animations to `Saved/HexPlanetHeroes/preview`) from two CC0 models of Quaternius that `fetch_rider_models.ps1` downloads to `Saved/ModelSources/hero` (`rider_model_sources.json`, about 3.1 MB, not committed): a horse, which keeps its skeleton and the animations it came with (`ACTIONS`), and a knight in armor, as the first man, a rogue baked stiff onto one bone, looked like a scarecrow to the developer, was not bound to the horse and did not move with it ("людина як опудало - має бути рицар", "людина має бути в дуеті з цим - синхронна до його анімацій"). The knight keeps his skeleton: its bones, named `Rider_<bone>`, join that of the horse under the bone of its back that carries him (`SEAT_BONE`), his meshes are reduced (`KNIGHT_RATIO`) and his own animations dropped, and in every frame of every animation of the horse the script poses him (`animate`): seated, each limb pointed along a direction in the world (`SEAT_POSE`, `aim`, towards the bone that hangs on it, as the tails of imported bones point anywhere, and turned into the space of the skeleton, which the importer turns), his back upright against `COUNTER` of how far the back of the horse tips (`SPINE`), leaning with how fast the seat rises (`LEAN`), his head level (`HEAD_COUNTER`), and alive, as he sat like a mannequin at first ("на ньому має бути айдл"): in an animation of `IDLE_FRAMES` or more he breathes (`BREATH`) and looks about (`HEAD_LOOK`, `HEAD_NOD`); the bones of his feet, which his model moves by targets, hang on his shins (`FEET`). His right hand holds the pole of his banner, which is bound to that hand, and his left the reins, strips from the mouth of the horse to that hand, each end bound to its own bone, the hand following the neck of the horse (`REINS`), as the developer asked ("рицар має тримати флаг-шток у руці, інша рука не видно поводій - висить у повітрі"): `make_extras` makes them around him as he sits on the horse at rest and takes each vertex back to where its bone rests. He has gloves in place of hands: the triangles of his hands are cut away (`HAND_BONES`) and a closed hand of leather, a rounded lump from each wrist over where the hand was, is bound to the wrist (`glove`, `GLOVE`; blocks of steel at first, which the developer asked away as grey cubes), the pole and the ends of the reins in them, as the hands of the model showed holes in the game, the pole stood beside an open hand, and fists that were turned upright around the pole in every frame turned around it by tens of degrees from frame to frame, so the banner shook ("руки диряві, рука та флаг шток не суміщені", "флаг труситься", "крутяться обидва кулачки"); now a hand is rigid with its forearm and the pole leans at most about 2 degrees in any animation, measured in Blender. The turn of every bone that is keyed keeps the sign of its quaternion from one frame to the next (`key_rotation`). The cloth of the banner waves, as the developer asked the same day ("флаг який несе герой має коливатись"): it is in `FLAG_STRIPS` strips whose vertices have a V of 7 in the only UV of the mesh and how far along the cloth they lie as their U, by which `M_HexPlanetHero` moves them across the cloth (`HERO_OFFSET_CODE`, `HeroFlagWave` units of the mesh at the far end, at `HeroFlagWaveSpeed`, of `DA_HexHeroes`). A saddle cloth rides on the seat bone. The script also makes one animation of the horse, `Look`, its idle one with its head turned to a side and back (`LOOK_BONES`, `LOOK_ANGLE`). About 5,700 triangles and 112 bones, without textures, the colors of the materials of the models in the colors of its vertices and the alpha 1 on what takes the color of the player (the cloth of the knight, `OWNER_MATERIALS`, the saddle cloth and the banner). `import_planet_heroes.py` imports it with its skeleton and animations to `/Game/HexPlanet/Heroes/SK_PlanetRider` (`import_rider`, which deletes that folder first, as a skeleton with other bones does not take the place of the one before, and is skipped while the file is missing). `UHexHeroAnimInstance` plays the animations without an animation blueprint: one sequence, looped or once, blended in from the one before over `AnimBlendTime` (`FHexHeroAnimProxy::Evaluate`), and after one played once the next, looped. `UpdateBody` chooses them (`FBodyState`): `IdleAnimation` while it stands, `StartAnimation` once as it sets out and then `MoveAnimation` (a gallop, its rate by the speed of the hero), `StopAnimation` from `StopAnimStart` of its length as it stops, and `ChosenAnimation` when it is chosen while it stands. The developer found the leaps the horse came with too much for setting out and stopping, and rearing, then a sway of the whole horse, too much for being chosen ("занаддто пригає - потрібно злегка", "прибрати наклон на тілі - тільки голова"), so it sets out and stops through a few steps of its walk and, chosen, only turns its head (`Look`), each at its own rate (`Look|Animation` of the config); one left empty is skipped. A class that names a `Mesh` of its own, or a project without the rider, draws a mesh that stands still as before, a `UStaticMeshComponent`: `SM_PlanetHero` with `M_HexPlanetHero` (`HERO_CODE` of `create_planet_assets.py`, whose argument `heroes` builds the materials of the heroes alone), the colors of its vertices and, by their alpha, the color of its player (`PlayerColor`) on its cloak, banner, plume and the cloth under its saddle, which also glow a little in it (`HeroGlow`). The mesh, a rider with a banner, comes from `Tools/HexPlanetHeroes/generate_planet_heroes.py` (`Saved/HexPlanetHeroes`), a placeholder for the mesh of an artist (`HeroMesh`, or `Mesh` of a class), and `import_planet_heroes.py`, run with the commandlet, imports it to `/Game/HexPlanet/Heroes` with the flat marks: the dot and the target, of radius 1, and the piece of the line of a way, one unit long and wide. `HeroScale` is 2.5 (1.8 until the developer asked for a larger hero on 2026-11-11), and a hero and its ring grow as the camera leaves them, from `FarStart` up to `FarScale` times, so they show from afar (`FHexHero::ViewScale`). The ring under the hero chosen (`M_HexPlanetHeroMark`, unlit, `RingColor`, the color of a way since the developer asked on 2026-11-12, the color of the player before, breathing by `RingPulse`, its bright band and dark rims with sharp edges, as with soft ones it looked blurred to them) is no mesh of an asset but a band laid on the ground around the hero (`BuildRing`, a `UProceduralMeshComponent` of the planet of its layer: `RingSegments` pieces at `RingRadius`, each across the band between the ground under its inner and its outer edge, of whichever cell lies there, laid again only when the hero moved or grew or the ground changed), as on 2026-11-11 the developer found a flat ring cut by the hill beside the hero; and the marks of a way are instances with `M_HexPlanetHeroPath`, the same graph, which tests the depth too since 2026-11-11, so a wall of the underground or the crown of a tree in front of a way hides it, and both hide behind the curve of the planet, where the line from the camera dips below the sea (`HERO_MARK_OPACITY_CODE`, `PlanetRadius` of `MPC_HexPlanet`), as the developer found on 2026-11-12 the ring and the way shining through the planet seen from its other side, whose ground the fog of war leaves undrawn, each drawn `PathDepthBias` nearer the camera along the view (`MarkDepthBias`), so bumps of the ground do not cut them, with `MarkOpacity` of its own; they grow from afar in the material (`HERO_MARK_OFFSET_CODE`), a piece of the line in its width alone (`MarkAlong`), or it would overshoot the marks at its ends. Each hero lights the map around itself, as the focus of the camera did before there were heroes and still does on a planet without any: `UHexHeroes::GetLights` gives the `MaxLights` heroes of a layer nearest that focus, at most 8 (`MaxHeroLights`, `HERO_LIGHTS` of the script), and `AHexPlanet::Tick` gives them to `UpdateHeroShade` on the surface (`HeroFocus0` to `HeroFocus7` and `HeroShade` of the haze, the water and the clouds, set only when they changed) and to `UpdateHeroLight` underground: a point light over each (`HeroLights`, made as they are needed), the `ShadowLights` (1) nearest with shadows, and `FogFocus0` to `FogFocus7` with `FogFocusOn` for the fog and `HeroLight0` to `HeroLight7` with `HeroLightOn` for the motes, so the fog clears and the dust shows around each; the shaders take the nearest light as a share of its reach (`hero_lights`), and the argument `lights` of the script builds those five materials alone. A layer whose heroes are all on the other one has no day at all but at its entrances and auroras. A light reaches `HeroLightReach` of its layer, as the focus did, or with `LightMoveShare` above 0 that share of the cells of plains the hero walks in a day, as the developer wanted one day (`hex.HeroLightShare` tries it while the game runs: 1 lights 10 cells around, where the layer has 4); a radius that changes makes the render state of its light again, as `SetAttenuationRadius` left the lights of the GPU scene stale (an ensure of the renderer). On the machine it was made on at 1280x720, after the frame time had settled: on the surface at 900 with two heroes the shade costs nothing that could be measured (3.68 / 3.67 ms of GPU with `hex.PlanetForcedLight` 0 and 1), and underground at Terra exit 112 at 1500 with two heroes the lights, the clear fog and the lit dust cost 0.22 ms (3.54 / 3.32 ms with `hex.PlanetHeroLight` 1 and 0), as one light with shadows did before, nearly all of it the shadows of the nearest (3.34 ms with `hex.PlanetHeroLight 2`), and a reach of 10 cells costs about 0.27 ms more (3.81 ms). Four heroes are placed on Small near the view saved there, two of Red and two of Blue, whom the game moves, so the heroes and the AI show at once; since 2026-11-12, as the developer asked for armies to start with, each starts with three stacks of its faction of the first levels (about 24 to 32 of the first, 10 to 12 of the second and 3 or 4 of the third or fourth), and its three castles are built to the middle (`buildhalf`) and guarded: those of Red and Blue by five stacks of up to level 5, upgraded on the lower levels, and the one of nobody by more of them.
- The players move in turns, one after the other, as the developer decided on 2026-11-11 ("ходи тільки послідовно"): those that have heroes, in the order of their index (`GetActivePlayers`), each on its turn (`TurnPlayer`, `BeginTurn`), and after the last of them the next day begins, when every hero has its points again and the mines give (`BeginDay`). End turn (Enter, the button of the panel) ends the turn of a person (`UHexHeroes::EndTurn`; `hex.Hero endturn` that of whoever moves, and outside the game it only begins the next day). A person plays on its own turn alone: it chooses and sends only its own heroes (`IsPersonTurn`, `CanChoose`, `Click`, `SelectNext`, `FindNextAble`), the hero of another player it clicks is only a cell something stands on, and the panel lists its heroes alone and says whose turn it is. The fog of war shown is that of the person whose turn it is, or was last (`ViewPlayer`, `GetShroudPlayer`), so with two persons it changes with the turn, as in a hot seat. A player is moved by a person or by the game (`IsPlayerAi`, `SetPlayerAi`), as the developer asked on 2026-11-11 for a primitive AI that runs about and opens the map: the Heroes tab of the palette has a switch Human / AI for each player under Who plays (`UHexPlanetPainter::TogglePlayerAi`), `hex.Hero ai <Player> [on|off]` does the same, and `heroes.json` keeps it, so changing it leaves the planet unsaved (`UpdateEdited`); it is no step of Undo. On the turn of such a player its heroes move one after the other (`UpdateAi`, `AiHeroId`), each until it has no more to do (`MoveAi`, `FHexHero::bAiDone`), and then its turn ends by itself: steps of neighbors spread from the ground its player has not explored, as far as a hero sees (underground from the passages alone), give the cells from which it would open some; of those it reaches today (`FindFieldFor`) it takes the one nearest that ground, the cheapest of them, so it walks along the edge of what it knows, else the cheapest it reaches on a later day, on either layer, as its ways go through the entrances and exits its player has seen from both sides, and with nothing left it goes through the nearest entrance or exit of its layer whose other side its player has not seen. Before it explores it gathers, as the developer asked on 2026-11-11 ("АІ - має почати збирати їх, гравець може захопити шахти - АІ захоче їх повернути"; `MoveAiToSite`, `UHexSites::GetGoal`, `EHexSiteGoal`): of the sites its player has seen it goes for a pile or a mill whose way with the visit (`GetSiteWayCost`) costs at most `AiPileReachDays` (1.5) days of its movement, a mine of nobody or of another player within `AiMineReachDays` (3) and a mine another player took from its own (`FHexSite::FormerOwners`) within `AiLostMineReachDays` (7), the one nearest by the share of its reach first, so a mine it lost comes before a pile half as far; a site another hero of its player goes for is left to that one (`FHexHero::AiSiteId`), and a hero whose points do not pay for the next step of a way of more than a day waits for tomorrow. The person hears of what such a hero does only where it sees it, and that a mine of its own was taken. On Small Blue took two sawmills, two quarries and the piles around them in five days, and took a sawmill back the turn after Red had taken it. What the person who watches does not see takes no time, as the developer asked: a hero under the fog of war takes its steps at once (`WalkUnseen`, `IsStepSeen`, `IsCellSeen`), so a whole turn out of sight is over within a frame, while one the fog does not hide on the layer that is not shown has the camera come to its layer first and sets out only then, as the developer asked on 2026-11-11 for the end of a turn given from the underground (`WaitForView`, which asks through `OnHeroSent` and waits for the trip at most `AiViewWaitMax`; until then a hero on the layer not shown moved unseen), while one the person sees (`IsSeen`) stands `AiThinkTime` before it sets out and walks at the speed of any hero, the camera with it (`GetFollowDirection`); where it goes into the fog the camera stays `AiFogHoldTime` (0.8 s) at the place it went in, before the game goes on with it or with the next hero, and where it is about to come out of the fog the camera goes there and stays as long before it does, the hero waiting meanwhile (`NoteSeen`, `FHexHero::bWasSeen`, `WatchHold`), as the developer asked on 2026-11-11 in place of a stop of the hero at the last cell seen, which was built first that day. Where it comes out of an entrance or exit into what the person sees the camera stays longer, `AiEntranceHoldTime` (2 s, `FHexHero::bCameThrough`), as the developer did not see an enemy come up out of an underground they had not explored before it took the castle beside the entrance; why 0.8 s did not show it was not found, so this is a guess that the camera came too late, and each such hold is logged. With no person in the game the players it moves take their turns a second apart (`AiTurnWait`). Nothing under the fog of war is drawn, as the developer asked: a hero the person does not see has no body and gives no light (`UpdateBody`, `GetLights`), and a chunk no cell of which shows, nor lies within two cells of one that does, draws neither its ground nor its objects nor its walls (`AHexPlanet::UpdateShroudedChunks`, `ShroudedChunks`, state 3 of `UpdateChunkLod`, state 4 of `UpdateWallLod`, `UHexPlanetObjects::SetHiddenChunks`; its pieces still stream, so a chunk that opens beside the camera needs no build on the game thread). Where nothing is drawn the materials of the fog take the level of the sea, or underground the tops of the passages, in place of the ground, so the bank and the dark look the same over a chunk that is drawn and one that is not; the boulders, floes, sites and markers of a planet are instances of the whole planet and are still drawn under it. On the machine it was made on at 1280x720 on Terra, early in a game, with the wisps and glows of the fog, measured with `hex.PlanetShroud` 1 and 0 after the frame time had settled, the fog now saves time where it cost some: close to a hero at 900 3.64 against 3.74 ms of GPU and 4.15 against 5.33 ms of the render thread (241 against 187 frames a second), at 4000 3.99 against 4.31 ms and 4.50 against 4.86, and underground at 1500 3.39 against 3.61 ms and 3.90 against 4.16.
- Mines, piles and mills stand on the planet as sites (`UHexSites` in `Source/HexHero/Hero`, an object of `UHexHeroes`, `GetSites`), as the developer's document of 2026-11-11 describes them (see the game concept). Every value is in `DA_HexSites` (`UHexSiteConfig`, `Content/HexPlanet/Sites`, which `import_planet_sites.py` creates only when missing): the resources (`FHexResourceInfo`: a name, a color and `StartAmount`, what a player starts with) and the kinds of sites (`FHexSiteKind`, by `EHexSiteType`): a mine of each resource with what it gives a day (`PerDay`), a pile of each with what it holds (`AmountMin` to `AmountMax`, a number by the site and the game; 500 to 1000 for gold is Claude's, as the document gives none), and a mill, whose resource is its own, any of the seven by the site and the game, and which gives `MillDays` (3) days of the income of the mine of that resource once a week. A site stands on a cell a hero could stand on, of either layer, one to a cell. A mine or a mill is a building that fills its cell (`FHexSiteKind::FillsCell`, `UHexSites::FindBlockerAt`), so no hero enters it (`UHexHeroes::GetCellCost`): a hero pointed to its cell walks to the cell beside it from which the whole way is the cheapest (`FindWay`) and there visits it (`VisitSite`, `FHexHero::VisitCell`), which costs what a step onto that cell would, and goes onto its cell, as the developer decided on 2026-11-11 ("всеж таки потрібно зайти на гекс з шахтою - це також стає охороною ресурса"; it took a mine from beside and stayed there before): it walks over `SiteWalkTime` of `DA_HexHeroes` to the place of the hero there (`FHexSiteKind::HeroPlace`, (0.6, 0) radii of a cell before the mesh; `UHexSites::GetHeroPoint`, which `UHexHeroes::GetStandDirection` gives for that cell, so its steps from there start at that place), which the meshes leave free, the mine and the mill standing in the back of their cells with a trodden patch before them (`HERO_PLACE` of the generator), so the meshes do not overlap. While it stands there it guards the site: one hero to a cell, so no other hero visits it (`GetSiteWayCost`, which gives no way then; "Lada guards it" at the mark and in the message), and nothing walks through the cell, which stays closed to ways. A hero sent to its own cell on a mill takes what the mill gives in a new week (`Send`). What it does at the site: a mine becomes that of the player of the hero, whose color its flag takes, and gives what it gives in a day at once, but once a week whoever takes it (`FHexSite::VisitedWeek`, as the developer asked on 2026-11-11: "коли захвачую шахту, вона дає свій ресурс в цей момент, якщо на цьому тижні його ще не забрали"), and a mill gives if it has not this week (`UHexSites::Visit`); that a mill is visited as a mine is, being a building, is Claude's. A pile lies on its cell and is walked onto, as the developer decided on 2026-11-11 ("ресурс одиночний - підбирається тільки якщо на нього наступити - не рядом"; until then it filled its cell and was taken from beside): the hero whose way ends on its cell takes what it holds, and the pile is gone (`UHexSites::FindPileAt`, `UHexHeroes::TakePile`, from `Arrive` where the hero stops on the cell it was sent to), while one that only runs over it on its way further leaves it, as the developer corrected the same day ("якщо герой пробігає через ресурс - він його не бере - тільки як кінцева ціль"); a hero that stopped on one for want of points takes it when sent to its own cell (`Send`), and no hero is placed on one (`AddHero`). Every new day each mine gives its owner its resource (`EndDay`, from `EndTurn`), and a new game owns no mine and has every pile (`Restart`). Guards, which must be beaten before a mine is taken, wait for battles; a mine changes hands with any visit by another player's hero, as the developer said on 2026-11-11, and remembers who lost it (`FormerOwners`), so a player the game moves comes to take it back. `sites.json` of a planet (`FHexSiteLine`: id, kind, layer, cell; `HexPlanetFile::SaveSites`, as `heroes.json`, the `HexHero.Hero.Sites` test) holds the sites, which the Mines tab of the palette on either layer (`EHexPaintTab::Sites`, `CaveSites`, `EHexPaintTool::Site`, `UHexPlanetPainter::PaintSite`) places with a click and takes away with Ctrl+click, each a step of Undo, and scatters them over both layers, as the developer asked on 2026-11-11 ("ресурси, потрібно створити/розкидати їх по світу"): Generate of that tab (`UHexPlanetPainter::GenerateSites`, `hex.PlanetPaint regenerate sites`, one step of Undo, each press another layout; Remove all beside it; `hex.Site generate [Seed]` and `clear` without Undo) puts `UHexSites::Generate` in place of all the sites there are. `HexSiteGenerator` (pure and deterministic, the `HexHero.Hero.SiteGenerator` test) places them by `Generation` of `DA_HexSites` (`FHexSiteGeneration`, the numbers Claude's): a site stands only where a hero could (`UHexHeroes::IsOpenGround`), on ground of at least `MinRegionCells` cells, on no pentagon, off the cells of entrances and of where heroes start with `EntranceClearRings` and `StartClearRings` around them, off a cell that holds a cover, a decor or an object placed by hand (`bAvoidObjects`; for an underground never shown read from its `objects.json`, `UHexPlanetObjects::FindSavedCells`), and where closing its cell cuts no way and leaves a cell to visit it from: the open cells around it lie in one run (`KeepsWays`). Two sites keep `Spacing` (3) cells apart, two mines `MineSpacing` (6) and two of one kind `SameMineSpacing` (14). First each player gets, within `StartMinSteps` to `StartMaxSteps` steps of a hero from its first hero, a mine of each kind marked `bNearStart` (the sawmill and the quarry) and `StartPiles` (3) piles, then each layer what it still lacks of each kind to its number, `PerThousand` or `UndergroundPerThousand` of `Shares` (`FHexSiteShare`, `MakeDefaultSiteShares`) to a thousand cells a hero can stand on there, times `Density`, the mines first: about 4 mines, 10 piles and a mill to a thousand cells of the surface, and underground no wood and no mills but twice the rare resources. Small has 28 sites (24 on the surface), saved in its `sites.json`, and Terra gets about 420 in 18 ms. Who owns what and what was gathered are not saved yet. The sites of a layer are instances, a component of the planet of that layer for each mesh (`Rebuild`, when a site or the ground changed; `Mesh` of a kind, else `MineMesh`, `PileMesh` or `MillMesh` of its type), drawn with `M_HexPlanetSite` (`SITE_CODE`, `SITE_GLOW_CODE` and `SITE_OFFSET_CODE` of `create_planet_assets.py`, built with the argument `heroes`, which takes the floats of `DA_HexSites` by name, `HexMaterialConfig::Apply`): the colors of the vertices and, by their alpha, what a pile holds glowing a little in its own color (about 0.3, `PileGlow`), the color of the resource on the heap of a mine and the sacks of a mill (about 0.5), and the color of the owner on the flag of a mine (1), both from the custom data of the instance and glowing a little (`SiteGlow`). The meshes are placeholders from `Tools/HexPlanetSites/generate_planet_sites.py` (`Saved/HexPlanetSites`): one mine for every resource, a mound of rock with a timbered mouth, a heap and a flag, a windmill, and a pile of its own for each resource, many things at once, as the developer asked on 2026-11-11 so nobody takes a resource for a bush or a lone stone of the decor ("візуально більше штучек - по типу багато ... можливо якийсь ефект навколо них"): an open chest and a heap of coins with stacks and ingots, a stack of logs between stakes with a block and an axe, a cart heaped with ore beside a heap of lumps and a pick, flasks of quicksilver on a tray, a heap of sulfur with lumps, sacks and a shovel, a cluster of red crystals, and a chest of cut gems spilled on a cloth, each on a patch of bare earth; `SM_Planet<Id>` is named after its kind, and `import_planet_sites.py` gives it to a kind of `DA_HexSites` that names no mesh (`SM_PlanetPile`, a heap of chunks in the color of the resource, stays for a kind without one). Around a pile sparkles twinkle and rise: stars of a few triangles in its mesh (vertex alpha about 0.75, their red when each twinkles, green how fast, blue how far it rises, their UV where a corner lies in the star), which the world position offset grows to `SparkleSize`, turns to the camera, lifts by `SparkleRise` and grows from afar (`SparkleSpeed`, `SparkleFarStart`, `SparkleFarScale`), lit by `SparkleBrightness` in the color of the resource `SparkleWhite` of the way to white, and kept tiny in the shadow pass, so they cast none. The developer found them too large and bright on 2026-11-11, so they are smaller (`SparkleSize` 3, 5 before), dimmer (4, 10 before), faster (`SparkleSpeed` 0.8), show for `SparkleShare` (0.6) of their time only and let what is behind them through: the materials of the sites are masked, and `SparkleOpacity` (0.55) of the pixels of a sparkle are kept by a noise of the pixel that changes every frame (`SITE_MASK_CODE`), so every site pays for a mask now, not only a pile that dissolves; that cost is not measured. `blender --background --factory-startup --python` with a small script that loads the GLB files and draws their vertex colors shows the shapes in seconds. A site sits on the ground under it (`UHexSites::FindSeat`), as on 2026-11-11 the developer found one on a hill hanging in the air, when it stood at the height of the middle of its cell alone: the ground is read in the middle and at six points `SeatReach` of the way to the sides of the cell out, the site leans as that ground does, a pile up to `PileMaxTilt` (28) degrees and a building up to `BuildingMaxTilt` (10), and stands between the lowest of that ground and its mean (`SeatLowShare`), `SiteSink` deeper, and every mesh goes on under its foot (`SKIRT` of the generator, the rock under a mine and a mill). The meshes have one LOD, as reduced a mine lost its timbers, heap and flag and read as a rock. What happens to a site shows, as the developer asked on 2026-11-11 ("підбор ресурса, чи захват шахти - має бути підкріплено ефектом, воно зникає не миттево а розчиняється, вспливає текст навколо піднятого ресурса з інформацією скільки чого підібрали"). The seventh custom value of an instance is when its mine was taken or its pile taken (`FHexSite::EventTime`, the seconds of the world, which the Time of a material counts too, so nothing is set every frame): a mine flashes, its flag and heap glowing `CaptureFlash` more and fading over `CaptureFlashTime`, and a pile that is taken, gone for the game at once, is drawn on while it dissolves (`FHexSite::bFading`, over `PickupFadeTime`, 0.9 s) with `M_HexPlanetSiteFade` (`FadeMaterial`, the same graph masked with `Fading` 1, `build_site_graph`, `SITE_MASK_CODE`, in a component of its own for each mesh, so only what dissolves pays for a mask): it goes in grains `PickupGrain` across by a hash of the place, those about to go glowing by `PickupGlow`, while it lifts by `PickupLift` and every sparkle shines, grows and flies up. What the hero gained floats up over the site as text (`FHexSitePopup`, `UHexSites::AddPopup` from `Visit` with `bShown`, drawn by `SHexHeroPanel::OnPaint` over the projected point): "+6 Crystals" in the color of the resource `PopupWhite` towards white, and for a mine "Quarry: Red" in the color of the player with "+2 Ore" after it (`PopupStagger`), for `PopupTime` seconds, rising `PopupRise` pixels and fading over its last third, only for what the person who watches sees. The seat of a site is found once until the ground changes (`FHexSite::Seat`, `bSeated`, `OnLayerRebuilt`), with the triangles of its cell read once for its seven points, as taking a pile or a mine places every site of the layer again. In a `-game` run the first pile taken draws the default material while the shaders of `M_HexPlanetSiteFade` compile, black for that second; `HighResShot` leaves out the text, `Shot showui` shows it. `hex.Site <list|add <Cell> <Kind>|remove <Cell>|generate [Seed]|clear>` places and lists them from the console, `list` with what each player has and gets a day, a cell by its number or `near:<Steps>[:<Side>]` from the hero chosen. Their GPU cost is not measured.
- Castles stand on the planet (`UHexCastles` in `Source/HexHero/Hero`, an object of `UHexHeroes`, `GetCastles`), as the developer described them on 2026-11-11 (see the game concept) and answered then: the panel of a castle at the right of the screen, closed by a click outside it; a guarded castle taken by weighing the armies until there are battles; a castle on three hexes with rounded edges; and numbers of Claude's to tune. Every value is in `DA_HexCastles` (`UHexCastleConfig`, `Content/HexPlanet/Castles`, which `import_planet_castles.py` creates only when missing): the factions (`FHexFaction`, `MakeDefaultFactions`), the two of the developer's document "Опис замків" under the working names it gives them, the Order of Dawn (`Dawn`: fewer, better and dearer creatures) and the Realm of Decay (`Decay`: more, weaker and cheaper ones), each with seven levels of creatures (`FHexCreature`: a basic and an upgraded `FHexCreatureStats` with Attack, Defense, damage, Health, Speed and gold, how many come to its dwelling a week, `Growth`, and for the seventh a rare resource each) and its buildings (`FHexBuilding`, by `EHexBuildingType`, each with levels, `FHexBuildingLevel`: a name, a cost by the Ids of the resources, the level of the hall and the fort it needs, and the mesh it shows from outside): the hall (village, town and city hall: 500, 1000 and 4000 gold a day, as the document of the resources says), the fort (fort, citadel, castle: the garrison counts 1.3, 1.6 and 2 times as strong and the dwellings offer 1, 1.5 and 2 times as many) and a dwelling for each level of creatures, which needs the dwelling before it, whose first level offers the basic creature and whose second the upgraded one. The numbers come from tables by level in `HexCastleConfig.cpp` times what tells the factions and the roles apart; the names of the creatures are those of the document in English (Sentinel and Shieldbearer to Sky warden and Eternal warden; Bone warrior and Bone guard to Bone serpent and Dusk serpent), the names of the buildings Claude's. A castle (`FHexCastle`) fills three cells of a layer that share a corner (`FindCells`, `OrderCells`: the lowest first, then counterclockwise), so no hero enters them (`UHexHeroes::GetCellCost`), and a hero beside it that is sent onto one of them visits it as a mine (`VisitSite`, `UHexCastles::Visit`), from the cell before its nearest gate whichever of its cells was clicked, as the developer asked on 2026-11-12 (`UHexCastles::GetGateCells`, the cheapest of them in `GetSiteWayCost`, `UHexHeroes::GetCastleEntryCell`), and beside any cell of it only when no gate is reached: into its own it goes in, for no points of movement, as the step out of it costs none either (`GetStepCost`, `FindFieldFor`), and the hero that guarded it comes out to where this one stood, so the two swap places, both as the developer asked on 2026-11-11 (`FHexHero::CastleId`, its cell the cell of the castle it went in by, from which its next way starts), stands in the middle of the yard (`GetGuardPoint`, `GuardPlace`, `UHexHeroes::PlaceHero`) and guards the castle, as the developer asked on 2026-11-11 ("герой який може зайти у замок - починає його охороняти": `FindGuard`, its army counting with the garrison in `GetDefense`, more by `HeroDefenseBonus` a point of its Defense), and the panel of the castle opens (`OnCastleOpened`); a hero that beats a castle goes in too, and the hero that guarded it is put out beside it without its army, which is Claude's until battles say what happens to a beaten hero. One of nobody or of another player is taken, at once when nothing guards it, else when the army of the hero (`GetAttack`: the power of its stacks, `FHexCreatureStats::GetPower`, more by `HeroAttackBonus` a point of Attack) is stronger than the garrison behind its walls (`GetDefense`), after which it loses the share of its creatures the garrison was of it to the power `LossPower`, at most `LossMax` (`Resolve`, `ApplyLosses`, the `HexHero.Hero.Castles` test), while a weaker attacker turns back without a loss; since 2026-11-12 a castle that a garrison or a hero defends is fought for in a battle instead (see the battles), and this weighing serves only the AI, which goes for a castle it would take so. A hero leads up to seven stacks (`FHexHero::Army`, `FHexStack`: faction, level, upgraded, count; `AddToArmy` joins a stack of its kind), and starts with the `army` of its line of `heroes.json` (`FHexHero::StartArmy`), else with `HeroStartWeeks` of what the first dwellings of the faction of its player offer (`MakeStartArmy`, `GetPlayerFaction`, that of its first castle); the panel of the heroes names the army of the one chosen. The owner of a castle gets the gold of its hall every day and builds `BuildsPerDay` (1) buildings a day, one a turn, as the developer confirmed on 2026-11-11 (`CanBuild`, `Build`, paid through `UHexSites::CanPay` and `Pay`), unless the cheat lifts that (`SetUnlimitedBuilds`, which the panel then says); on the first day of a week each dwelling offers its growth more (`BeginDay`, `GetGrowth`; a new castle offers a week of it, and a dwelling just built its first at once), which the owner hires for their price (`Hire`, `GetHireMax`) into the garrison or into the army of a hero of its own that stands beside the castle (`FindVisitor`), who may also swap whole stacks with the garrison (`MoveStack`). A castle of nobody is guarded by `NeutralGarrisonWeeks` of what its dwellings offer, unless its line of `castles.json` gives its garrison. A player the game moves builds what it can, the dwellings first, and hires everything into the garrison at the start of its turn (`RunAi`), and its heroes go for a castle of another that their army can take within `AiCastleReachDays` (`MoveAiToSite`), so on Small Blue took a castle guarded by 23 sentinels and one of nobody in two days. `castles.json` of a planet (`FHexCastleLine`: id, faction, layer, the three cells, the owner when a game starts, -1 for nobody, and since 2026-11-12 `built`, the level of each building by its Id, and `garrison`, its stacks as the `army` of a hero, what it has when a game starts, written when set; a line a castle, written by `UHexCastles::Save` with the heroes) holds the castles; `hex.Castle start <Castle|all>` (`KeepAsStart`) makes what a castle has built and its garrison now what it starts with, so a save keeps it, while what was built and hired in a game is not saved yet. The Castles tab of the palette on either layer (`EHexPaintTab::Castles`, `CaveCastles`, `EHexPaintTool::Castle`, `UHexPlanetPainter::PaintCastle`; underground since the developer asked on 2026-11-13, and only where every neighbor of its three cells is a passage, so its wall, which reaches past its cells, stands on the floor of a hall and not over the void, which is Claude's: the corridors of Small are two cells wide, so a hall is carved first with Passage of the Maze tab at a size of 3) puts a castle of the faction and the owner chosen on the cell clicked and the two around its corner nearest the cursor, and takes one away with Ctrl+click, each a step of Undo (`FHexPaintStep::OldCastles`); the planet draws no object on the cells of a castle (`AHexPlanet::UpdateClearCells`), and the generation of the sites keeps off them. `hex.Castle <list|add <Cell> <Faction> [Player]|remove <Cell>|build <Castle> <Building>|hire <Castle> <Level 1-7> <Count|max> [Hero]|move <Castle> <Hero> <tohero|togarrison> <Slot>|open <Castle>|give <Player> <Resource> <Amount>>` drives them from the console, `list` with the armies of the heroes and what they count. `hex.Cheat <resources [Amount] [Player]|build [on|off]|buildall [Castle|all]>` is for trying the game, as the developer asked on 2026-11-11: `resources` gives the person who plays, or the player named, that much of every resource, or without an amount `CheatGold` and `CheatResources` of `DA_HexCastles`, `build` lets every castle build without the limit of a day, or switches that, and `buildall [Castle|all]`, which the developer asked for the same day, builds every building to its last level for nothing (`UHexCastles::BuildAll`), in the castle named, else in the one chosen, else in every castle of the person who plays, and with `all` in every castle; each says so on the screen. Since 2026-11-12, as the developer asked, `units <Faction> <Level 1-7> <Count> [up] [Hero]` gives the hero chosen, or the one named, that many creatures of that level of the faction, upgraded with `up` (`UHexHeroes::AddUnits`, joined to a stack of their kind, refused when its seven slots are full), and `buildhalf <Castle|all>` builds a castle, the one chosen without a name, to the middle (`UHexCastles::BuildHalf`): the hall and the fort to the middle of their levels, rounded up, the dwellings of the four lower levels whole and that of the fifth begun, its creatures offered at once. A castle is chosen with the hero that guards it, as the developer proposed on 2026-11-11 ("якщо герой у замку, та обрано замок - це те саме що й обрано героя - команда на мув виведе героя і обере його"), after a first rule of that day, which chose either a hero or a castle and told the two apart by a click on the body of the hero, was not clear to play (`UHexHeroes::SelectCastle`, which chooses its guard too, `ChooseHero`, which chooses the castle of a hero that is in one, `GetSelectedCastleId`; `GetSelected` gives the guard of the castle chosen and no hero for a castle without one, and `Update` keeps the two together): a click on a castle of the person, or on the body of the hero in it, chooses both (`AHexPlanetPlayerController::ClickGame`), a click on a cell then shows the way of that hero and a second click, the key or the right button sends it out, after which it is chosen alone (`StartNextStep`), and two clicks on the castle, or the key that sends a hero while no way is shown (`WalkHero`), open its panel. Until then a hero that had left a castle was not chosen again by a click on it after a click on the castle, as `Click` took it for chosen already.
